  <script>
    const appShell = document.getElementById('appShell');
    const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
    const resizeHandle = document.getElementById('resizeHandle');
    const collapseBtn = document.getElementById('collapseBtn');
    const hamburgerBtn = document.getElementById('hamburgerBtn');
    const pageTitle = document.getElementById('pageTitle');
    const pageSubtitle = document.getElementById('pageSubtitle');
    const navItems = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item'));
    const pages = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.page'));
    const statusBox = document.getElementById('statusBox');
    const chatWindow = document.getElementById('chatWindow');
    const questionInput = document.getElementById('questionInput');
    const savedConfigPreview = document.getElementById('savedConfigPreview');

    const STORAGE_KEYS = {
      sidebarWidth: 'cb_sidebar_width',
      sidebarCollapsed: 'cb_sidebar_collapsed',
      activePage: 'cb_active_page',
      llmConfig: 'cb_llm_config'
    };

    const PAGE_META = {
      'llm-config': { title: 'LLM Configuration', subtitle: 'Configure the LLM provider — the LangGraph pipeline rebuilds automatically on save.' },
      chat: { title: 'Chat', subtitle: 'Six LangGraph nodes: Intent Checker → Schema Checker → SQL Writer (LLM) → SQL Review → SQL Validator → Executor.' }
    };

    // Maps a LangGraph stage string prefix to the display name of the stage pill.
    // Prefixes must match the strings emitted by each agent node exactly:
    //   intent_checker.py    → "intent_checker:…"
    //   schema_checker.py    → "schema_checker:…"
    //   sql_writer_agent.py  → "sql_writer_agent:…"
    //   sql_reviewer.py      → "sql_reviewer:…"
    //   sql_validator.py     → "sql_validator:…"
    //   executor_agent.py    → "executor_agent:…"
    const STAGE_PREFIX_MAP = [
      { prefix: 'intent_checker',   label: 'Intent Checker'  },
      { prefix: 'schema_checker',   label: 'Schema Checker'  },
      { prefix: 'sql_writer_agent', label: 'SQL Writer'      },
      { prefix: 'sql_reviewer',     label: 'SQL Review'      },
      { prefix: 'sql_validator',    label: 'SQL Validator'   },
      { prefix: 'executor_agent',   label: 'Executor'        },
      { prefix: 'clarify_node',     label: 'Clarification'   },
    ];

    /**
     * Highlight the stage pills that appear in the live LangGraph stage trace.
     * Each pill's data-prefix attribute is compared against every stage string
     * returned by the orchestrate endpoint; a match lights the pill up in accent blue.
     */
    function updateStagePills(stages) {
      const trail = document.getElementById('stageTrail');
      if (!trail) return;
      const pills = trail.querySelectorAll('.stage');
      pills.forEach(pill => {
        pill.style.background = '';
        pill.style.color = '';
        pill.style.borderColor = '';
        pill.style.fontWeight = '';
      });
      STAGE_PREFIX_MAP.forEach(({ prefix, label }, idx) => {
        const matched = stages.some(s => s.startsWith(prefix));
        if (!matched) return;
        const pill = pills[idx];
        if (!pill) return;
        pill.style.background = '#2563eb';
        pill.style.color = '#ffffff';
        pill.style.borderColor = '#1d4ed8';
        pill.style.fontWeight = '600';
      });
    }

    function setSidebarWidth(width) {
      const min = 220;
      const max = 420;
      const clamped = Math.min(max, Math.max(min, width));
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--sidebar-width', `${clamped}px`);
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarWidth, String(clamped));
    }

    function setCollapsed(collapsed) {
      appShell.classList.toggle('sidebar-collapsed', collapsed);
      collapseBtn.textContent = collapsed ? '▶' : '◀';
      collapseBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', collapsed ? 'Expand sidebar' : 'Collapse sidebar');
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarCollapsed, String(collapsed));
    }

    function saveConfigLocally(config) {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.llmConfig, JSON.stringify(config));
      savedConfigPreview.textContent = JSON.stringify(config, null, 2);
    }

    function loadConfigLocally() {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.llmConfig);
      if (!saved) {
        savedConfigPreview.textContent = 'No saved configuration yet.';
        return null;
      }
      const config = JSON.parse(saved);
      document.getElementById('modelName').value = config.model_name || 'google/gemma-4-31b-it';
      document.getElementById('dlhBaseUrl').value = config.dlh_base_url || '';
      document.getElementById('dlhApiKey').value = config.dlh_api_key || '';
      savedConfigPreview.textContent = JSON.stringify(config, null, 2);
      return config;
    }

    function renderMarkdown(text) {
      const escaped = text
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
        .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
        .replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
      return escaped
        .replace(/```([\s\S]*?)```/g, '<pre class="code-block">$1</pre>')
        .replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>')
        .replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, '<code>$1</code>')
        .replace(/\n/g, '<br>');
    }

    function addMessage(text, role, meta, persist = true) {
      const msg = document.createElement('div');
      msg.className = `msg ${role}`;
      msg.innerHTML = role === 'bot' ? renderMarkdown(text) : text;
      chatWindow.appendChild(msg);

      if (meta) {
        const metaBox = document.createElement('div');
        metaBox.className = 'meta markdown';
        metaBox.innerHTML = meta;
        chatWindow.appendChild(metaBox);
      }

      chatWindow.scrollTop = chatWindow.scrollHeight;

      if (persist) {
        chatWindow.dataset.hasConversation = 'true';
      }
    }

    function clearChat() {
      chatWindow.innerHTML = '';
      delete chatWindow.dataset.hasConversation;
      updateStagePills([]); // reset all pills to default (unlit) state
    }

    function setActivePage(pageId) {
      navItems.forEach((item) => item.classList.toggle('active', item.dataset.page === pageId));
      pages.forEach((page) => page.classList.toggle('active', page.dataset.page === pageId));
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.activePage, pageId);
      pageTitle.textContent = PAGE_META[pageId].title;
      pageSubtitle.textContent = PAGE_META[pageId].subtitle;
      if (window.innerWidth <= 920) {
        appShell.classList.remove('mobile-sidebar-open');
      }
    }

    async function connectProvider() {
      const btn = document.getElementById('connectBtn');
      btn.disabled = true;

      // Show spinning dots while connecting
      statusBox.className = 'status testing';
      statusBox.innerHTML =
        '<div class="status-dots"><span></span><span></span><span></span></div>' +
        '<span>Testing connection\u2026</span>';

      const payload = {
        provider: 'dlh',
        model_name: document.getElementById('modelName').value,
        dlh_base_url: document.getElementById('dlhBaseUrl').value,
        dlh_api_key: document.getElementById('dlhApiKey').value,
      };

      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/v1/config/llm', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify(payload)
        });
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || data.detail || 'Connection failed');
        statusBox.className = 'status success';
        statusBox.textContent = data.message;
        saveConfigLocally(payload);
        addMessage(`Connected to ${data.provider}. LangGraph pipeline is ready.`, 'bot');
        checkBackendHealth();
        setActivePage('chat');
      } catch (error) {
        statusBox.className = 'status error';
        statusBox.textContent = error.message;
      } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
      }
    }

    function showTypingIndicator() {
      const el = document.createElement('div');
      el.className = 'typing-indicator';
      el.id = 'typingIndicator';
      el.innerHTML = '<span></span><span></span><span></span>';
      chatWindow.appendChild(el);
      chatWindow.scrollTop = chatWindow.scrollHeight;
    }

    function hideTypingIndicator() {
      const el = document.getElementById('typingIndicator');
      if (el) el.remove();
    }

    // Render text as markdown (bold + code + pre + newlines).
    function formatNoticeText(text) {
      return text
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
        .replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>')
        .replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, '<code>$1</code>')
        .replace(/\n/g, '<br>');
    }

    // Append a styled notice card (blocked = red, clarify = blue) to the chat.
    function addNotice(text, type) {
      const el = document.createElement('div');
      el.className = `msg-notice ${type}`;
      el.innerHTML = formatNoticeText(text);
      chatWindow.appendChild(el);
      chatWindow.scrollTop = chatWindow.scrollHeight;
      chatWindow.dataset.hasConversation = 'true';
    }

    async function sendMessage() {
      const question = questionInput.value.trim();
      if (!question) return;

      const sendBtn = document.getElementById('sendBtn');
      addMessage(question, 'user');
      questionInput.value = '';
      sendBtn.disabled = true;
      sendBtn.textContent = 'Sending\u2026';
      showTypingIndicator();

      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ question })
        });
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || data.detail || 'Chat failed');

        // Update stage pills with live LangGraph trace.
        updateStagePills(data.stages || []);

        const stageTrace = (data.stages || []).join(' → ');
        const stageHtml = `<div style="font-size:12px;color:#57606a;margin-bottom:10px;word-break:break-all">${stageTrace || '—'}</div>`;

        // ── Clarification requested (vague or ambiguous question) ──────────
        if (data.needs_clarification) {
          hideTypingIndicator();
          addNotice(data.clarification_question, 'clarify');
          return;
        }

        // ── Pipeline error (blocked keyword, max retries, etc.) ────────────
        if (data.error) {
          hideTypingIndicator();
          addNotice(data.error, 'blocked');
          return;
        }

        // ── Successful query result ────────────────────────────────────────
        const rowCount = Array.isArray(data.df_output) ? data.df_output.length : 0;
        const summary  = data.result_summary || `${rowCount} row(s) returned.`;
        const resultsJson = JSON.stringify(data.df_output || [], null, 2);
        const encodedData = encodeURIComponent(resultsJson);
        hideTypingIndicator();
        addMessage(
          'Query completed successfully.',
          'bot',
          `<strong>Agent Trace</strong>${stageHtml}` +
          `<strong>Summary</strong><br>${summary.replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g,'<strong>$1</strong>').replace(/`([^`]+)`/g,'<code>$1</code>')}<br><br>` +
          `<strong>SQL</strong><pre class="code-block">${(data.sql_query || 'N/A').replace(/</g,'&lt;')}</pre>` +
          `<br><strong>Rows returned:</strong> ${rowCount}<br><br>` +
          `<strong>Results</strong><pre class="code-block" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">${resultsJson}</pre>` +
          `<button class="csv-download-btn" data-csv="${encodedData}" onclick="downloadCsv(this)">&#8595; Download as CSV</button>`
        );
      } catch (error) {
        hideTypingIndicator();
        addNotice('An unexpected error occurred. Please try again.', 'blocked');
      } finally {
        sendBtn.disabled = false;
        sendBtn.textContent = 'Send';
      }
    }

    function downloadCsv(btn) {
      let rows;
      try { rows = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(btn.dataset.csv)); } catch (_) { return; }
      if (!Array.isArray(rows) || rows.length === 0) { alert('No data to download.'); return; }
      const headers = Object.keys(rows[0]);
      const escape  = v => {
        const s = v === null || v === undefined ? '' : String(v);
        return s.includes(',') || s.includes('"') || s.includes('\n')
          ? `"${s.replace(/"/g, '""')}"` : s;
      };
      const csvContent = [
        headers.map(escape).join(','),
        ...rows.map(r => headers.map(h => escape(r[h])).join(','))
      ].join('\r\n');
      const blob = new Blob([csvContent], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
      const url  = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a    = document.createElement('a');
      a.href     = url;
      a.download = `results_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,19).replace(/[:T]/g,'-')}.csv`;
      a.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    }

    function getConversationEntries() {
      return Array.from(chatWindow.querySelectorAll('.msg, .meta'));
    }

    function copyChat() {
      const transcript = getConversationEntries()
        .map((entry) => entry.innerText.trim())
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join('\n\n');
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(transcript || 'No conversation available.');
    }

    function exportChat() {
      const transcript = getConversationEntries()
        .map((entry) => entry.innerText.trim())
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join('\n\n');
      const blob = new Blob([transcript || 'No conversation available.'], { type: 'text/plain' });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = url;
      a.download = 'conversation.txt';
      a.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    }

    navItems.forEach((item) => {
      item.addEventListener('click', () => setActivePage(item.dataset.page));
      item.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
        if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
          event.preventDefault();
          setActivePage(item.dataset.page);
        }
      });
    });

    document.getElementById('connectBtn').addEventListener('click', connectProvider);
    document.getElementById('loadConfigBtn').addEventListener('click', loadConfigLocally);
    document.getElementById('sendBtn').addEventListener('click', sendMessage);
    document.getElementById('clearBtn').addEventListener('click', clearChat);
    document.getElementById('copyChatBtn').addEventListener('click', copyChat);
    document.getElementById('exportChatBtn').addEventListener('click', exportChat);

    questionInput.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
      if (event.key === 'Enter' && !event.shiftKey) {
        event.preventDefault();
        sendMessage();
      }
    });

    collapseBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      setCollapsed(!appShell.classList.contains('sidebar-collapsed'));
    });

    hamburgerBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      appShell.classList.toggle('mobile-sidebar-open');
    });

    let resizing = false;
    resizeHandle.addEventListener('mousedown', () => {
      if (appShell.classList.contains('sidebar-collapsed') || window.innerWidth <= 920) return;
      resizing = true;
      document.body.style.cursor = 'col-resize';
    });

    document.addEventListener('mousemove', (event) => {
      if (!resizing) return;
      setSidebarWidth(event.clientX);
    });

    document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
      if (!resizing) return;
      resizing = false;
      document.body.style.cursor = 'default';
    });

    // ── Backend health polling ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    const backendStatus = document.getElementById('backendStatus');
    const backendLabel  = document.getElementById('backendLabel');

    async function checkBackendHealth() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch('/health', { method: 'GET', signal: AbortSignal.timeout(4000) });
        if (res.ok) {
          backendStatus.className = 'backend-status alive';
          backendLabel.textContent = 'Backend Active';
        } else {
          throw new Error('non-ok');
        }
      } catch (_) {
        backendStatus.className = 'backend-status dead';
        backendLabel.textContent = 'Backend Offline';
      }
    }

    checkBackendHealth();
    setInterval(checkBackendHealth, 8000);
    // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    // ── One-time migration: remove ICA fields from any previously saved config ──
    (function migrateStoredConfig() {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.llmConfig);
      if (!raw) return;
      try {
        const cfg = JSON.parse(raw);
        const { ica_base_url, ica_api_key, ica_namespace, ica_send_temperature, ...rest } = cfg;
        rest.provider = 'dlh';
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.llmConfig, JSON.stringify(rest));
      } catch (_) {}
    })();
    // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    const savedWidth = Number(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarWidth) || '280');
    setSidebarWidth(savedWidth);
    setCollapsed(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarCollapsed) === 'true');
    const savedPage = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.activePage) || 'llm-config';
    setActivePage(savedPage);
    loadConfigLocally();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
"""


class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    """Serve the UI and proxy /api/v1/* to the FastAPI backend."""

    # Silence the default per-request access log lines.
    def log_message(self, fmt: str, *args) -> None:  # noqa: N802
        pass

    def _send(self, code: int, content_type: str, body: bytes) -> None:
        try:
            self.send_response(code)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", content_type)
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(body)
        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionAbortedError, ConnectionResetError):
            # Client closed the connection before the response was sent
            # (e.g. browser tab closed, LLM took too long).  Nothing to do.
            pass

    # Serve the HTML page, static assets and proxy GET API calls.
    def do_GET(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802
        path = self.path.split("?")[0]

        if path in ("/", "/index.html"):
            body = HTML.encode("utf-8")
            self._send(200, "text/html; charset=utf-8", body)
            return

        # Serve font files from application/fonts/
        if path.startswith("/fonts/"):
            file_path = (_FONTS_DIR / path[len("/fonts/"):]).resolve()
            # Prevent path traversal outside fonts dir
            if not str(file_path).startswith(str(_FONTS_DIR)):
                self._send(403, "text/plain", b"Forbidden")
                return
            if file_path.is_file():
                data = file_path.read_bytes()
                ct = "font/truetype" if file_path.suffix in (".ttf", ".otf") else "application/octet-stream"
                self._send(200, ct, data)
            else:
                print(f"[font] 404 — file not found: {file_path}")
                self._send(404, "text/plain", b"Not found")
            return

        # Proxy GET API calls and the backend health probe
        if path.startswith("/api/") or path == "/health":
            self._proxy_get(path)
            return

        self._send(404, "text/plain", b"Not found")

    def _proxy_get(self, path: str) -> None:
        try:
            url = BACKEND_URL + path
            with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=15) as resp:
                body = resp.read()
                self._send(resp.status, resp.headers.get("Content-Type", "application/json"), body)
        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionAbortedError, ConnectionResetError):
            pass  # client already disconnected — nothing to send
        except Exception as exc:
            err = json.dumps({"error": str(exc)}).encode()
            self._send(502, "application/json", err)

    # Proxy all POST /api/* requests directly to the FastAPI backend.
    def do_POST(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802
        path = self.path.split("?")[0]
        if not path.startswith("/api/"):
            self._send(404, "text/plain", b"Not found")
            return

        length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
        raw_body = self.rfile.read(length) if length else b""

        target_url = BACKEND_URL + path
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            target_url,
            data=raw_body,
            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
            method="POST",
        )
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=120) as resp:
                body = resp.read()
                self._send(resp.status, resp.headers.get("Content-Type", "application/json"), body)
        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionAbortedError, ConnectionResetError):
            pass  # client disconnected before response arrived — nothing to send
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
            body = exc.read()
            self._send(exc.code, exc.headers.get("Content-Type", "application/json"), body)
        except Exception as exc:
            err = json.dumps({"error": str(exc)}).encode()
            self._send(502, "application/json", err)


def main() -> None:
    print("CDSW_APP_PORT =", os.getenv("CDSW_APP_PORT"))
    print("CDSW_READONLY_PORT = ", os.getenv("CDSW_READONLY_PORT"))
    print("CB_APP_PORT =", os.getenv("CB_APP_PORT"))
    print("UI_PORT =", UI_PORT)
    print("CDSW_PUBLIC_PORT =", os.getenv("CDSW_PUBLIC_PORT"))
    
    server = HTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", UI_PORT), Handler)
    url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{UI_PORT}"
    print(f"  Compliance Bot UI  →  {url}")
    print(f"  Backend proxy      →  {BACKEND_URL}")
    print("  Press Ctrl+C to stop.\n")

    # Open browser after a short delay so the server is ready
    threading.Timer(0.8, lambda: webbrowser.open(url)).start()

    try:
        server.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nStopped.")
        sys.exit(0)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


####################################################################################################
FILE: data\seed_synthetic_data.py
####################################################################################################

"""
Synthetic data generator for COMP_TBL.

Creates the database file, creates the table if absent, and inserts
realistic term-deposit compliance records into
``data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db``.  Run from the project root:

    python data/seed_synthetic_data.py              # create + insert 2000 rows
    python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --reset      # drop all rows then insert
    python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --count 500  # custom row count
    python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --count 0    # create DB/table only, no rows

The script uses only the Python standard library — no external packages needed.

Modular structure
-----------------
  ensure_database(db_path)          → creates the .db file and parent directories
  ensure_table(conn)                → creates COMP_TBL if it does not exist
  reset_table(conn)                 → deletes all existing rows
  generate_rows(count, start_i)     → returns a list of synthetic row tuples
  insert_rows(conn, rows)           → bulk-inserts rows
  print_summary(conn, db_path, n)   → prints statistics after insert
  seed(db_path, count, reset)       → orchestrates all of the above
"""

import argparse
import random
import sqlite3
from datetime import date, timedelta
from pathlib import Path

# DDL and INSERT SQL kept here so schema is defined exactly once.

_CREATE_TABLE_SQL = """
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS COMP_TBL (
    BRCD               TEXT,
    CUST_NBR           TEXT,
    CUST_NBR_CHKDGT    TEXT,
    ACCT_NBR           TEXT,
    ACCT_NBR_CHKDGT    TEXT,
    ACCT_BRNCH         TEXT,
    BRTH_DT            TEXT,
    AGE_YRS            INTEGER,
    SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG   INTEGER,
    KYC_FLG            TEXT,
    END_OF_DAY_BAL     REAL,
    ACCT_OPN_DT        TEXT,
    MTRTY_DT           TEXT,
    tenor_yr           INTEGER,
    tenor_day          INTEGER,
    MAT_DT             TEXT,
    NEW_EFFCTV_RT      REAL,
    PRDCT_ID           TEXT,
    PF_FAC_REPT        TEXT,
    PRODUCT_NAME       TEXT,
    BRANCHNAME         TEXT,
    CIRCLENAME         TEXT,
    CIRCLECD           TEXT,
    EXPECTED_EFFCTV_RT REAL,
    ISMOD              TEXT
)
"""

_INSERT_SQL = """
INSERT INTO COMP_TBL (
    BRCD, CUST_NBR, CUST_NBR_CHKDGT, ACCT_NBR, ACCT_NBR_CHKDGT, ACCT_BRNCH,
    BRTH_DT, AGE_YRS, SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG, KYC_FLG, END_OF_DAY_BAL, ACCT_OPN_DT,
    MTRTY_DT, tenor_yr, tenor_day, MAT_DT, NEW_EFFCTV_RT, PRDCT_ID, PF_FAC_REPT,
    PRODUCT_NAME, BRANCHNAME, CIRCLENAME, CIRCLECD, EXPECTED_EFFCTV_RT, ISMOD
) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
"""

_REQUIRED_COLUMNS = {
    "BRCD", "CUST_NBR", "CUST_NBR_CHKDGT", "ACCT_NBR", "ACCT_NBR_CHKDGT",
    "ACCT_BRNCH", "BRTH_DT", "AGE_YRS", "SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG", "KYC_FLG",
    "END_OF_DAY_BAL", "ACCT_OPN_DT", "MTRTY_DT", "tenor_yr", "tenor_day",
    "MAT_DT", "NEW_EFFCTV_RT", "PRDCT_ID", "PF_FAC_REPT", "PRODUCT_NAME",
    "BRANCHNAME", "CIRCLENAME", "CIRCLECD", "EXPECTED_EFFCTV_RT", "ISMOD",
}

# Static lookup pools for circles, branches and products used when generating rows.

_CIRCLES: list[tuple[str, str]] = [
    ("MUMBAI METRO", "MM01"),
    ("MUMBAI METRO", "MM02"),
    ("MUMBAI METRO", "MM03"),
    ("DELHI",        "DL01"),
    ("DELHI",        "DL02"),
    ("LUCKNOW",      "LK01"),
    ("LUCKNOW",      "LK02"),
    ("CHENNAI",      "CH01"),
    ("CHENNAI",      "CH02"),
    ("KOLKATA",      "KO01"),
    ("KOLKATA",      "KO02"),
    ("HYDERABAD",    "HY01"),
    ("BENGALURU",    "BN01"),
    ("BENGALURU",    "BN02"),
    ("AHMEDABAD",    "AH01"),
    ("PUNE",         "PN01"),
    ("JAIPUR",       "JP01"),
    ("BHOPAL",       "BP01"),
    ("CHANDIGARH",   "CG01"),
    ("PATNA",        "PT01"),
]

_BRANCHES: dict[str, list[str]] = {
    "MM01": ["VIKRAM SARABHAI BHAWAN", "FORT MUMBAI", "BANDRA WEST", "ANDHERI EAST"],
    "MM02": ["MANDVI MUMBAI", "DADAR EAST", "KURLA WEST", "BORIVALI"],

_PRODUCTS: list[tuple[str, str]] = [
    ("P100", "PUB IND UNI 181D-10YRS"),
    ("P101", "TDTDR PUB IND UNI-91-179D-INR"),
    ("P102", "SENIOR TERM DEPOSIT"),

    ("P110", "MEDIUM TERM DEPOSIT 180D-1YR"),
]

# Step 1: create the .db file and its parent directory if they do not exist.

def ensure_database(db_path: Path) -> None:
    """
    Create the parent directory and SQLite database file if they do not exist.

    This is a no-op when both already exist — SQLite creates the file on
    first ``connect()`` so the function just guarantees the directory is there.

    Args:
        db_path: Absolute or relative path to the ``.db`` file.
    """
    db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    # SQLite creates the file on connect; touching it here just makes the
    # "created" message accurate.
    existed = db_path.exists()
    # open and close immediately to create the file
    sqlite3.connect(str(db_path)).close()
    if not existed:
        print(f"[create] Database file created: {db_path}")
    else:
        print(f"[ok]     Database file exists:  {db_path}")

# Step 2: create COMP_TBL with the correct 25-column schema if it is missing or stale.

def ensure_table(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    """
    Create ``COMP_TBL`` with the full 25-column schema if it does not exist.

    If the table already exists but is missing required columns (stale schema),
    it is dropped and recreated so the seeder can always run safely.

    Args:
        conn: Open SQLite connection to the target database.
    """
    table_exists = conn.execute(
        "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='COMP_TBL'"
    ).fetchone()

    if table_exists:
        existing_cols = {
            row[1] for row in conn.execute("PRAGMA table_info(COMP_TBL)").fetchall()
        }
        if not _REQUIRED_COLUMNS.issubset(existing_cols):
            conn.execute("DROP TABLE COMP_TBL")
            conn.commit()
            print("[migrate] COMP_TBL schema was stale — dropped and will be recreated.")
            table_exists = None  # fall through to CREATE

    if not table_exists:
        conn.execute(_CREATE_TABLE_SQL)
        conn.commit()
        print("[create] COMP_TBL created.")
    else:
        print("[ok]     COMP_TBL already exists with correct schema.")

# Step 3 (optional): wipe all existing rows when --reset is passed.

def reset_table(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    """
    Delete every row from ``COMP_TBL``.

    Args:
        conn: Open SQLite connection to the target database.
    """
    deleted = conn.execute("DELETE FROM COMP_TBL").rowcount
    conn.commit()
    print(f"[reset]  Deleted {deleted:,} existing rows from COMP_TBL.")

# Step 4: pure row-factory helpers — no database access, only random value generation.

def _rand_date(start: date, end: date) -> date:
    """Return a random date between *start* and *end* (inclusive)."""
    return start + timedelta(days=random.randint(0, (end - start).days))

def _fmt(d: date) -> str:
    return d.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

def _rand_rate(base: float) -> float:
    """Return a rate within ±0.75 pp of *base*, rounded to 2 dp."""
    return round(base + random.uniform(-0.75, 0.75), 2)

def _make_row(i: int) -> tuple:
    """Generate one synthetic COMP_TBL row."""
    circle_name, circle_cd = random.choice(_CIRCLES)
    branch_name            = random.choice(_BRANCHES[circle_cd])
    prdct_id, product_name = random.choice(_PRODUCTS)

    age     = random.randint(18, 85)
    sr_flag = 1 if age >= 60 else 0
    dob     = _fmt(_rand_date(date(2025 - age - 1, 1, 1), date(2025 - age, 12, 31)))

    kyc     = "Y" if random.random() < (0.80 if sr_flag else 0.90) else "N"
    balance = round(random.lognormvariate(11.5, 1.2), 2)
    balance = max(10_000.0, min(5_000_000.0, balance))

    open_dt   = _rand_date(date(2005, 1, 1), date(2024, 6, 30))
    tenor_yr  = random.choice([1, 2, 3, 5, 7, 10, 15])
    tenor_day = random.randint(0, 364)
    mat_dt_d  = open_dt + timedelta(days=tenor_yr * 365 + tenor_day)
    mat_dt    = _fmt(mat_dt_d)

    is_mod  = "YES" if random.random() < 0.15 else "NO"
    mtrty_dt = _fmt(mat_dt_d + timedelta(days=random.randint(-30, 30))) if is_mod == "YES" else mat_dt

    base_rate = 7.0 if sr_flag else 6.5
    new_rate  = _rand_rate(base_rate)
    exp_rate  = _rand_rate(base_rate + 0.25)
    pf_fac    = "Y" if random.random() < 0.30 else "N"
    brcd      = f"{random.randint(1, 999):03d}"
    acct_brnch = f"M{random.randint(1, 99):03d}"

    return (
        brcd,                        # BRCD
        f"C{i:06d}",                 # CUST_NBR
        str(random.randint(1, 9)),   # CUST_NBR_CHKDGT
        f"A{i:06d}",                 # ACCT_NBR
        str(random.randint(1, 9)),   # ACCT_NBR_CHKDGT
        acct_brnch,                  # ACCT_BRNCH
        dob,                         # BRTH_DT
        age,                         # AGE_YRS
        sr_flag,                     # SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG
        kyc,                         # KYC_FLG
        balance,                     # END_OF_DAY_BAL
        _fmt(open_dt),               # ACCT_OPN_DT
        mtrty_dt,                    # MTRTY_DT
        tenor_yr,                    # tenor_yr
        tenor_day,                   # tenor_day
        mat_dt,                      # MAT_DT
        new_rate,                    # NEW_EFFCTV_RT
        prdct_id,                    # PRDCT_ID
        pf_fac,                      # PF_FAC_REPT
        product_name,                # PRODUCT_NAME
        branch_name,                 # BRANCHNAME
        circle_name,                 # CIRCLENAME
        circle_cd,                   # CIRCLECD
        exp_rate,                    # EXPECTED_EFFCTV_RT
        is_mod,                      # ISMOD
    )

def generate_rows(count: int, start_i: int) -> list[tuple]:
    """
    Generate *count* synthetic row tuples starting from index *start_i*.

    ``start_i`` is used for ``CUST_NBR`` / ``ACCT_NBR`` sequential IDs so
    appending rows never collides with existing ones.

    Args:
        count:   Number of rows to generate.
        start_i: Index of the first new row (1-based).

    Returns:
        List of row tuples ready for ``executemany``.
    """
    return [_make_row(start_i + i) for i in range(count)]

# Step 5: bulk-insert the generated rows in a single executemany call.

def insert_rows(conn: sqlite3.Connection, rows: list[tuple]) -> None:
    """
    Bulk-insert *rows* into ``COMP_TBL`` using a single ``executemany``.

    Args:
        conn: Open SQLite connection (must already have COMP_TBL).
        rows: Row tuples as returned by :func:`generate_rows`.
    """
    conn.executemany(_INSERT_SQL, rows)
    conn.commit()
    print(f"[insert] {len(rows):,} rows inserted into COMP_TBL.")

# Step 6: query the DB and print row counts and top circles after the insert.

def print_summary(conn: sqlite3.Connection, db_path: Path, inserted: int) -> None:
    """
    Print a statistics summary after the insert.

    Args:
        conn:     Open SQLite connection.
        db_path:  Path to the database file (for display only).
        inserted: Number of rows that were just inserted.
    """
    total   = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL").fetchone()[0]
    sr      = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL WHERE SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG = 1").fetchone()[0]
    kyc_n   = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL WHERE KYC_FLG = 'N'").fetchone()[0]
    circles = conn.execute(
        "SELECT CIRCLENAME, COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL GROUP BY CIRCLENAME ORDER BY 2 DESC LIMIT 5"
    ).fetchall()

    print(f"\n{'='*55}")
    print(f"  Database : {db_path}")
    print(f"  Inserted : {inserted:,} rows")
    print(f"  Total    : {total:,} rows in COMP_TBL")
    print(f"{'='*55}")
    print(f"  Senior citizens  (SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG=1) : {sr:,}")
    print(f"  KYC non-compliant (KYC_FLG='N')        : {kyc_n:,}")
    print(f"\n  Top 5 circles by row count:")
    for name, cnt in circles:
        print(f"    {name:<22} {cnt:>5}")
    print(f"{'='*55}\n")

# Orchestrator that runs all six steps in sequence.

def seed(db_path: Path, count: int, reset: bool) -> None:
    """
    Full end-to-end pipeline:

    1. :func:`ensure_database` — create ``.db`` file and parent dirs if absent.
    2. :func:`ensure_table`    — create ``COMP_TBL`` if absent or stale.
    3. :func:`reset_table`     — (only when ``reset=True``) delete all rows.
    4. :func:`generate_rows`   — build synthetic row tuples in memory.
    5. :func:`insert_rows`     — bulk-insert into the database.
    6. :func:`print_summary`   — display post-insert statistics.

    Args:
        db_path: Path to the SQLite ``.db`` file.
        count:   Number of rows to insert (0 = create DB/table only).
        reset:   When ``True``, delete all existing rows before inserting.
    """
    # Step 1 — database file
    ensure_database(db_path)

    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path))

    try:
        # Step 2 — table schema
        ensure_table(conn)

        # Step 3 — optional reset
        if reset:
            reset_table(conn)

        if count == 0:
            print("[info]   --count 0: skipping row insert.")
            print_summary(conn, db_path, inserted=0)
            return

        # Step 4 — generate rows (start_i avoids CUST_NBR / ACCT_NBR collisions)
        start_i = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL").fetchone()[0] + 1
        rows = generate_rows(count, start_i)

        # Step 5 — insert
        insert_rows(conn, rows)

        # Step 6 — summary
        print_summary(conn, db_path, inserted=count)

    finally:
        conn.close()

# CLI argument parser and script entry-point.

def _parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=(
            "Create the SQLite database + COMP_TBL schema and insert synthetic data.\n"
            "Safe to re-run: creates only what is missing."
        ),
        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--count", type=int, default=2000,
        help="Number of rows to insert (default: 2000). Use 0 to create DB/table only.",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--reset", action="store_true",
        help="Delete all existing rows before inserting.",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--db", type=Path,
        default=Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "comp_db" / "COMP_REPORTS.db",
        help="Path to the SQLite database file.",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--seed", type=int, default=42,
        help="Random seed for reproducibility (default: 42).",
    )
    return p.parse_args()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    args = _parse_args()
    random.seed(args.seed)
    seed(db_path=args.db, count=args.count, reset=args.reset)

# Adding a New Database or Table

The pipeline is designed so that **adding a new SQLite database requires editing only one file**:

```
genai/config/databases.toml
```

No Python code changes are needed. The `db_registry` module reads this TOML file at runtime and the pipeline picks up new entries automatically on next startup.

---

## How it works

```
databases.toml
     │
     ▼
genai/utils/db_registry.py   (loads at startup, cached in memory)
     │
     ├── SchemaAgent         (merges TOML column descriptions into schema context)
     └── SQLWriterAgent      (reads table description + columns + few-shots for the prompt)
```

The `SchemaAgent` always introspects the live SQLite file using `PRAGMA table_info` — so the column list is always accurate. The TOML column descriptions add **business-friendly explanations** that ground the LLM (e.g., `KYC_FLG = "FLAG IS Y IF KNOW-YOUR-CUSTOMER DOCUMENTATION IS COMPLETE, OTHERWISE N"`).

---

## Step-by-step: Add a new database

### Step 1 — Place the `.db` file

Put your SQLite database file inside `data/comp_db/` (or any sub-folder under `data/`):

```
data/
└── comp_db/
    ├── COMP_REPORTS.db   ← existing
    └── LOANS.db          ← new
```

### Step 2 — Open `genai/config/databases.toml`

Scroll to the bottom of the file (below the `ADD NEW DATABASES BELOW THIS LINE` comment) and add a new block.

### Step 3 — Add the database block

```toml
[databases.LOANS_DB]
db_file     = "data/comp_db/LOANS.db"
description = "Retail loan origination and servicing database."
```

- The key `LOANS_DB` is the **database stem** — used for logging and registry lookups. It must be unique. Use `UPPER_SNAKE_CASE`.
- `db_file` is the **relative path** from the project root.
- `description` is used internally; it does not appear in the LLM prompt.

### Step 4 — Add a table block

```toml
[databases.LOANS_DB.tables.LOAN_TBL]
description  = "Loan accounts with EMI and overdue tracking."
filter_query = "SELECT * FROM LOAN_TBL WHERE BRANCH_ID = '{branch_id}'"
```

- The key `LOAN_TBL` must match the exact table name in SQLite (case-sensitive).
- `description` appears in the LLM prompt as the table summary.
- `filter_query` is optional. It is stored for reference but not yet used by the pipeline automatically.

### Step 5 — Add column descriptions

```toml
[databases.LOANS_DB.tables.LOAN_TBL.columns]
LOAN_ID      = "UNIQUE LOAN IDENTIFIER"
CUST_ID      = "CUSTOMER IDENTIFIER LINKED TO THE LOAN"
BRANCH_ID    = "BRANCH WHERE THE LOAN WAS ORIGINATED"
LOAN_AMT     = "ORIGINAL PRINCIPAL AMOUNT OF THE LOAN IN INR"
EMI_AMT      = "MONTHLY EQUATED MONTHLY INSTALMENT AMOUNT"
OVERDUE_FLG  = "FLAG Y IF LOAN IS OVERDUE BY MORE THAN 30 DAYS, OTHERWISE N"
LOAN_TYPE    = "TYPE OF LOAN — HOME, PERSONAL, VEHICLE, BUSINESS"
DISBURSED_DT = "DATE THE LOAN WAS DISBURSED TO THE CUSTOMER"
```

> **Tip:** Only include columns you want the LLM to know about. You do not need to list every column — PRAGMA introspection will show all columns anyway. The descriptions here add semantic meaning.

### Step 6 — Add few-shot examples

```toml
[databases.LOANS_DB.tables.LOAN_TBL.few_shots]
examples = """
Question: List all overdue loans.
Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT * FROM LOAN_TBL WHERE OVERDUE_FLG = 'Y';
<end_sql_response>

Question: How many home loans were disbursed in 2024?
Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT COUNT(*) AS loan_count FROM LOAN_TBL WHERE LOAN_TYPE = 'HOME' AND DISBURSED_DT LIKE '2024%';
<end_sql_response>

Question: What is the total loan amount by branch?
Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT BRANCH_ID, SUM(LOAN_AMT) AS total_loan_amt FROM LOAN_TBL GROUP BY BRANCH_ID;
<end_sql_response>
"""
```

> **Tip:** Provide 3–6 examples covering different query patterns: COUNT, SUM, WHERE filters, date comparisons, GROUP BY. The LLM learns from these examples.

### Step 7 — Restart the application

```bash
python application/Launcher.py
```

The registry reloads on startup. Verify the new table is registered:

```bash
python -c "from genai.utils.db_registry import get_all_tables, list_databases; print(list_databases(), get_all_tables())"
```

Expected output:
```
['COMP_REPORTS', 'LOANS_DB']  ['COMP_TBL', 'LOAN_TBL']
```

---

## Step-by-step: Add a new table to an existing database

If your new table lives in an existing database (e.g., `COMP_REPORTS.db`), just add the table block inside the existing database section:

```toml
# Existing database block
[databases.COMP_REPORTS]
db_file     = "data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db"
description = "Banking compliance reports database for term-deposit products."

# Existing table
[databases.COMP_REPORTS.tables.COMP_TBL]
...

# ← Add your new table here
[databases.COMP_REPORTS.tables.BRANCH_MASTER]
description  = "Master list of all bank branches with address and region details."

[databases.COMP_REPORTS.tables.BRANCH_MASTER.columns]
BRCD         = "BRANCH CODE — UNIQUE 4-DIGIT IDENTIFIER"
BRANCHNAME   = "FULL BRANCH NAME"
CITY         = "CITY WHERE THE BRANCH IS LOCATED"
REGION       = "ADMINISTRATIVE REGION CODE"
ACTIVE_FLG   = "Y IF BRANCH IS CURRENTLY ACTIVE, N IF CLOSED"

[databases.COMP_REPORTS.tables.BRANCH_MASTER.few_shots]
examples = """
Question: List all active branches in Mumbai.
Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT * FROM BRANCH_MASTER WHERE ACTIVE_FLG = 'Y' AND UPPER(CITY) LIKE '%MUMBAI%';
<end_sql_response>
"""
```

---

## Updating column descriptions for an existing table

Simply edit the values under `[databases.<DB>.tables.<TABLE>.columns]` in `databases.toml`:

```toml
# Before
KYC_FLG = "FLAG IS Y IF COMPLETE, OTHERWISE N"

# After (more detailed)
KYC_FLG = "KYC COMPLIANCE FLAG — Y = KNOW-YOUR-CUSTOMER DOCUMENTATION VERIFIED AND COMPLETE; N = DOCUMENTATION MISSING OR EXPIRED"
```

Restart the server. The updated description will be injected into the next LLM prompt.

---

## Updating few-shot examples

Edit the `examples` string under `[databases.<DB>.tables.<TABLE>.few_shots]`. You can add, remove, or rewrite examples freely. The pipeline reads them fresh on every restart.

---

## Hot-reload without restart

If you need to refresh the registry at runtime (e.g., in a long-running CDSW session), call:

```python
from genai.utils.db_registry import reload
reload()
```

This clears the in-memory cache. The next API request will re-read `databases.toml`.

---

## Full example — complete new database block

```toml
# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Database: LOANS_DB  (data/comp_db/LOANS.db)
# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

[databases.LOANS_DB]
db_file     = "data/comp_db/LOANS.db"
description = "Retail loan origination and servicing database."

[databases.LOANS_DB.tables.LOAN_TBL]
description  = "Loan accounts with EMI and overdue tracking."
filter_query = "SELECT * FROM LOAN_TBL WHERE BRANCH_ID = '{branch_id}'"

[databases.LOANS_DB.tables.LOAN_TBL.columns]
LOAN_ID      = "UNIQUE LOAN IDENTIFIER"
CUST_ID      = "CUSTOMER IDENTIFIER LINKED TO THE LOAN"
BRANCH_ID    = "BRANCH WHERE THE LOAN WAS ORIGINATED"
LOAN_AMT     = "ORIGINAL PRINCIPAL AMOUNT OF THE LOAN IN INR"
EMI_AMT      = "MONTHLY EMI AMOUNT"
OVERDUE_FLG  = "FLAG Y IF LOAN IS OVERDUE BY MORE THAN 30 DAYS, OTHERWISE N"
LOAN_TYPE    = "TYPE OF LOAN — HOME, PERSONAL, VEHICLE, BUSINESS"
DISBURSED_DT = "DATE THE LOAN WAS DISBURSED TO THE CUSTOMER"

[databases.LOANS_DB.tables.LOAN_TBL.few_shots]
examples = """
Question: List all overdue loans.
Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT * FROM LOAN_TBL WHERE OVERDUE_FLG = 'Y';
<end_sql_response>

Question: How many home loans were disbursed in 2024?
Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT COUNT(*) AS loan_count FROM LOAN_TBL WHERE LOAN_TYPE = 'HOME' AND DISBURSED_DT LIKE '2024%';
<end_sql_response>
"""
```

---

## Troubleshooting

| Symptom | Check |
|---|---|
| New table not found | Verify the TOML key matches the exact SQLite table name (case-sensitive) |
| Wrong database selected | Add the table name as a keyword in your question; SchemaAgent scores by overlap |
| LLM ignoring column descriptions | Restart the server to clear the registry cache |
| Import error for `tomllib` | Run `pip install tomli` (required for Python < 3.11) |
| Registry shows 0 tables | Check `databases.toml` for TOML syntax errors with `python -c "import tomllib; tomllib.load(open('genai/config/databases.toml','rb'))"` |



####################################################################################################
FILE: docs\api-reference.md
####################################################################################################

# API Reference

## Base URL

```
http://localhost:8000
```

---

## Endpoints

### `GET /health`

Liveness probe. Returns immediately without touching the database or LLM.

**Response `200`:**

```json
{"status": "ok", "version": "2.0.0"}
```

---

### `POST /api/v1/config/llm`

Configure the active LLM provider and rebuild the LangGraph pipeline. Must be called before the first compliance query if the default `.env` credentials are not set.

**Request body:**

```json
{
  "provider": "dlh",
  "model_name": "google/gemma-4-31b-it",
  "dlh_base_url": "http://your-dlh-host:9000/api/v1",
  "dlh_api_key": "EMPTY",
  "dlh_verify_ssl": false,
  "ica_base_url": "https://api.nextgen-beta.ica.ibm.com",
  "ica_api_key": "",
  "ica_namespace": "chat-models",
  "ica_send_temperature": false
}
```

| Field | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `provider` | Yes | — | `"dlh"` or `"ica"` |
| `model_name` | No | from `.env` | Remote model name or ICA resource ID |
| `dlh_base_url` | No | from `.env` | DLH inference server base URL |
| `dlh_api_key` | No | from `.env` | DLH bearer token |
| `dlh_verify_ssl` | No | `false` | Verify DLH TLS certificate |
| `ica_base_url` | No | from `.env` | ICA API host URL |
| `ica_api_key` | No | from `.env` | ICA developer API key |
| `ica_namespace` | No | `"chat-models"` | ICA namespace |
| `ica_send_temperature` | No | `false` | Forward temperature/max_tokens to ICA |

**Response `200`:**

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "provider": "dlh",
  "message": "LLM connection successful. LangGraph pipeline is ready."
}
```

**Response `500` (connection failed):**

```json
{"error": "Connection test failed: ..."}
```

---

### `POST /api/v1/compliance/chat`

Run the NL2SQL pipeline and return a simplified response (no stage trace).

**Request body:**

```json
{
  "question": "How many accounts have KYC flag N?",
  "question_id": "req-001"
}
```

| Field | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `question` | Yes | Natural-language compliance question |
| `question_id` | No | Optional idempotency key; auto-generated UUID if omitted |

**Response `200`:**

```json
{
  "question_id": "req-001",
  "sql_query": "SELECT COUNT(*) AS record_count FROM COMP_TBL WHERE KYC_FLG = 'N' LIMIT 10;",
  "df_output": [{"record_count": 267}],
  "result_columns": ["record_count"],
  "result_summary": "Result: **record_count** = `267`",
  "error": null
}
```

**Response `503`** (pipeline not initialised):

```json
{"detail": "Compliance processor is not initialised. Please retry in a few seconds."}
```

---

### `POST /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate`

Run the full LangGraph pipeline and return the complete orchestration response including every agent stage.

**Request body:** same as `/chat`

**Response `200`:**

```json
{
  "question_id": "7f3c9d1a-2b5e-4c89-8f1d-3a6b0e9c7d42",
  "interpreted_question": "How many persons from the Mumbai circle?",
  "needs_clarification": false,
  "clarification_question": null,
  "sql_query": "SELECT COUNT(*) AS record_count FROM COMP_TBL WHERE UPPER(CIRCLENAME) LIKE '%MUMBAI%' LIMIT 10;",
  "df_output": [{"record_count": 306}],
  "result_columns": ["record_count"],
  "result_summary": "Result: **record_count** = `306`",
  "stages": [
    "intent_agent:ok",
    "schema_agent:db=COMP_REPORTS.db",
    "sql_writer_agent:llm_generated retry=0",
    "sql_review_agent:passed",
    "sql_validator_agent:passed dialect=sqlite",
    "executor_agent:ok rows=1"
  ],
  "error": null
}
```

**Response fields:**

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `question_id` | string | Echo of the request ID (or auto-generated UUID) |
| `interpreted_question` | string | Normalised question after intent analysis |
| `needs_clarification` | bool | `true` when the user must provide more detail |
| `clarification_question` | string \| null | Message shown when `needs_clarification = true` |
| `sql_query` | string \| null | The generated (and canonicalised) SQL statement |
| `df_output` | array \| null | Result rows as `[{"column": value, ...}, ...]` |
| `result_columns` | array | Column names in order |
| `result_summary` | string \| null | Human-readable summary of the results |
| `stages` | array | Ordered audit trail of completed stage names |
| `error` | string \| null | Error message when the pipeline failed |

---

## Stage string reference

The `stages` array records every completed node. Each entry has the format `<node_name>:<detail>`.

| Stage string | Node | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `intent_agent:ok` | IntentAgent | Intent is clear; pipeline proceeds |
| `intent_agent:empty` | IntentAgent | Empty question |
| `intent_agent:too_short` | IntentAgent | Question too short or meaningless |
| `intent_agent:clarification_required` | IntentAgent | Vague or ambiguous question |
| `intent_agent:disallowed_action_word=DELETE` | IntentAgent | Write keyword detected in question |
| `clarify_node:terminal` | ClarifyNode | Terminal — clarification returned to user |
| `schema_agent:db=COMP_REPORTS.db` | SchemaAgent | Selected database |
| `sql_writer_agent:llm_generated retry=0` | SQLWriterAgent | SQL generated on first attempt |
| `sql_writer_agent:llm_generated retry=1` | SQLWriterAgent | SQL regenerated after first review failure |
| `sql_writer_agent:extraction_failed` | SQLWriterAgent | LLM output missing delimiter tags |
| `sql_review_agent:passed` | SQLReviewAgent | All safety and schema checks passed |
| `sql_review_agent:disallowed_keyword=DROP` | SQLReviewAgent | Disallowed keyword in LLM SQL |
| `sql_review_agent:no_select` | SQLReviewAgent | SQL does not start with SELECT |
| `sql_review_agent:missing_from` | SQLReviewAgent | SQL missing FROM clause |
| `sql_review_agent:unknown_tables=[...]` | SQLReviewAgent | Referenced table not in database |
| `sql_review_agent:max_retries_exceeded` | SQLReviewAgent | Retry limit reached |
| `sql_validator_agent:passed dialect=sqlite` | SQLValidatorAgent | sqlglot AST validation passed |
| `sql_validator_agent:parse_error` | SQLValidatorAgent | sqlglot could not parse the SQL |
| `sql_validator_agent:disallowed_stmt_type=...` | SQLValidatorAgent | Non-SELECT statement detected |
| `sql_validator_agent:skipped_no_sqlglot` | SQLValidatorAgent | sqlglot not installed; check skipped |
| `executor_agent:ok rows=1` | ExecutorAgent | Successful execution; N rows returned |
| `executor_agent:empty_sql` | ExecutorAgent | No SQL to execute |
| `executor_agent:db_not_found` | ExecutorAgent | Database file missing |
| `executor_agent:error` | ExecutorAgent | SQLite execution error |

---

## Error scenarios

### Clarification (blue card)

```json
{
  "needs_clarification": true,
  "clarification_question": "Your question is a bit unclear...",
  "sql_query": null,
  "df_output": null,
  "stages": ["intent_agent:clarification_required", "clarify_node:terminal"],
  "error": null
}
```

### Blocked write keyword (red card)

```json
{
  "needs_clarification": false,
  "clarification_question": null,
  "sql_query": null,
  "df_output": null,
  "stages": ["intent_agent:disallowed_action_word=DELETE", "clarify_node:terminal"],
  "error": null
}
```

> Note: When a write keyword is blocked by the Intent Agent, the message is in `clarification_question` (not `error`). When blocked by the SQL Review Agent (in LLM-generated SQL), it appears in `error`.

### Pipeline error

```json
{
  "sql_query": "...",
  "df_output": null,
  "stages": ["...", "sql_review_agent:max_retries_exceeded"],
  "error": "The request could not be completed after 3 attempts..."
}
```

---

### Full pipeline query

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8000/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"question": "How many senior citizens are in the Thane branch?"}'
```

### Simplified query

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8000/api/v1/compliance/chat \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"question": "List the top 10 accounts by balance", "question_id": "my-req-001"}'
```

### Configure ICA provider

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8000/api/v1/config/llm \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "provider": "ica",
    "model_name": "your-resource-id",
    "ica_api_key": "your-ica-key",
    "ica_namespace": "chat-models"
  }'
```

# Architecture — Compliance Chatbot v2

> **LangGraph-powered NL2SQL compliance query engine for banking term-deposit products.**
> Six specialised agents collaborate to analyse intent, retrieve the live database schema,
> generate, review, validate, and execute every SQL query — with a strict read-only guarantee
> at every layer.

---

## Table of Contents

1. [Three-Layer Architecture Overview](#three-layer-architecture-overview)
2. [Project Directory Layout (accurate)](#project-directory-layout)
3. [Process Architecture — How it Starts](#process-architecture--how-it-starts)
4. [Six-Node LangGraph Pipeline](#six-node-langgraph-pipeline)
   - [Node 1 — IntentChecker](#node-1--intentchecker)
   - [Node 2 — SchemaChecker](#node-2--schemachecker)
   - [Node 3 — SQLWriterAgent](#node-3--sqlwriteragent-only-llm-call)
   - [Node 4 — SQLReviewer](#node-4--sqlreviewer)
   - [Node 4.5 — SQLValidator](#node-45--sqlvalidator)
   - [Node 5 — ExecutorAgent](#node-5--executoragent)
5. [AgentState — Shared Pipeline State](#agentstate--shared-pipeline-state)

## Three-Layer Architecture Overview

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  PROCESS LAYER  (root + application/)                                │
│                                                                      │
│  run_ui.py ─────────── Standalone HTTP proxy (stdlib only)          │
│  application/Launcher.py ─ Process manager (starts backend + UI)    │
│  application/main.py ───── FastAPI ASGI application                 │
│  application/run_ui.py ─── (same proxy, runs from project root)     │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  GENAI LAYER  (genai/)                                               │
│                                                                      │
│  genai/core/           LLM lifecycle, schemas, processor, exceptions │
│  genai/nl2sql_agent/   Six-node LangGraph StateGraph pipeline        │
│  genai/utils/          Config, logging, registry, helpers            │
│  genai/config/         TOML: app, db, llm, logging, query, ui       │
│  genai/prompts/        TOML: intent, schema, sql_writer, review …   │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  DATA LAYER  (data/)                                                 │
│                                                                      │
│  data/comp_db/         SQLite database (COMP_REPORTS.db)            │
│  data/logs/            Rotating log file (compliance_bot.log)       │
│  data/uploads/         User-uploaded files (future)                 │
│  data/reports/         Generated report outputs                     │
│  data/exports/         CSV / data exports                           │
│  data/cache/           Request or query cache                       │
│  data/temp/            Temporary working files                      │
│  data/sample_data/     Reference / seed data                        │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## Project Directory Layout

```
COMPLIANCE-CHATBOT-DLH/                  ← Project root
│
├── run_ui.py                            ← Root-level UI proxy entry-point
│                                          (serves the chat HTML; proxies
│                                           API requests to the FastAPI backend)
│
├── application/
│   ├── Launcher.py                      ← Cross-platform process manager
│   │                                      Spawns backend + UI as sub-processes
│   │                                      Watchdog loop monitors both
│   │                                      OS-aware: bind host, Ctrl+C, SIGTERM
│   ├── main.py                          ← FastAPI ASGI app (uvicorn loads this)
│   │                                      Endpoints: /health, /api/v1/config/llm,
│   │                                      /api/v1/compliance/chat,
│   │                                      /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate, /
│   ├── run_ui.py                        ← Same proxy as root run_ui.py
│   └── fonts/                           ← Urbanist typeface (18 weight .ttf files)
│
├── genai/
│   │
│   ├── config/                          ← All TOML configuration (no secrets)
│   │   ├── application.toml             ← App name, host, port, debug flag
│   │   ├── database.toml                ← DB folder and filename defaults
│   │   ├── databases.toml               ★ TABLE REGISTRY — add new DBs here
│   │   │                                  Defines per-table: description,
│   │   │                                  columns, filter_query, few-shots
│   │   ├── llm.toml                     ← LLM provider defaults
│   │   ├── logging.toml                 ← Log handlers, levels, rotation policy
│   │   ├── query.toml                   ★ ROW-LIMIT POLICY
│   │   │                                  enforce_default_limit, default_limit,
│   │   │                                  max_display_rows
│   │   └── ui.toml                      ← UI proxy backend URL override
│   │
│   ├── prompts/                         ← All user-facing strings (no LLM strings
│   │   │                                   hardcoded in Python)
│   │   ├── intent_checker.toml          ← IntentChecker messages
│   │   ├── schema_checker.toml          ← SchemaChecker headers + sample_row_limit
│   │   ├── sql_writer.toml              ← System role, rules, prompt template,
│   │   │                                   retry feedback header
│   │   ├── sql_review.toml              ← SQLReviewer feedback + user error strings
│   │   ├── compliance.toml              ← Legacy compliance messages
│   │   ├── report_generation.toml       ← Report output templates
│   │   ├── system.toml                  ← Global system messages
│   │   └── common.toml                  ← Shared SQL delimiter tags
│   │
│   ├── core/
│   │   ├── exceptions.py                ← Domain exception hierarchy
│   │   │                                   ComplianceBotError (base)
│   │   │                                   ModelLoadError, ModelInferenceError,
│   │   │                                   LLMProviderError, LLMTimeoutError,
│   │   │                                   UnknownProviderError,
│   │   │                                   SqlGenerationError, SqlExecutionError,
│   │   │                                   DatabaseConnectionError,
│   │   │                                   RecordNotFoundError,
│   │   │                                   DataIngestionError, SchemaValidationError
│   │   ├── model.py                     ← LLM provider lifecycle + inference
│   │   │                                   ModelLoaderService (DLH / ICA)
│   │   │                                   create_model_service() factory
│   │   │                                   generate_with_retry() helper
│   │   │                                   ProviderManager (multi-provider registry)
│   │   │                                   EmbeddingService stub (reserved for RAG)
│   │   ├── processor.py                 ← ComplianceProcessor
│   │   │                                   Orchestration bridge:
│   │   │                                   request → graph.invoke() → response
│   │   │                                   Also contains COMP_SCHEMA_DETAILS
│   │   │                                   (legacy Python schema reference dict)
│   │   ├── schemas.py                   ← Pydantic API models (single source of truth)
│   │   │                                   ComplianceChatRequest / Response
│   │   │                                   ComplianceOrchestrationResponse
│   │   │                                   LLMConnectionRequest / Response
│   │   │                                   HealthResponse
│   │   │                                   + response factory functions
│   │   └── __init__.py
│   │
│   ├── nl2sql_agent/
│   │   ├── state.py                     ← AgentState TypedDict
│   │   │                                   Shared mutable dict flowing through
│   │   │                                   all six LangGraph nodes
│   │   ├── graph.py                     ← StateGraph factory (build_nl2sql_graph)
│   │   │                                   Wires nodes + conditional edges
│   │   │                                   Routing functions: _route_after_intent,
│   │   │                                   _route_after_reviewer,
│   │   │                                   _route_after_validator
│   │   ├── intent_checker.py            ← Node 1: IntentChecker (rule-based, no LLM)
│   │   ├── schema_checker.py            ← Node 2: SchemaChecker (PRAGMA + TOML)
│   │   ├── sql_writer_agent.py          ← Node 3: SQLWriterAgent (★ LLM call)
│   │   │                                   ContextBuilder assembles full prompt
│   │   │                                   optimise() SQL clean-up helpers
│   │   ├── sql_reviewer.py              ← Node 4: SQLReviewer (regex + PRAGMA)
│   │   │                                   RetryHandler manages retry count
│   │   ├── sql_validator.py             ← Node 4.5: SQLValidator (sqlglot AST)
│   │   ├── executor_agent.py            ← Node 5: ExecutorAgent (read-only SQLite)
│   │   │                                   format_summary, to_markdown_table,
│   │   │                                   to_csv, extract_scalar helpers
│   │   ├── main.py                      ← CLI entry-point (standalone debug run)
│   │   └── __init__.py
│   │
│   ├── utils/
│   │   ├── config.py                    ← AppSettings (Pydantic BaseSettings)
│   │   │                                   DatabaseSettings, ModelSettings
│   │   │                                   SQL_BEGIN_TAG / SQL_END_TAG constants
│   │   │                                   COMPLIANCE_TABLE_NAME constant
│   │   │                                   get_settings() singleton
│   │   ├── logger.py                    ← Centralised logging factory
│   │   │                                   get_logger(name) — configure once,
│   │   │                                   log everywhere consistently
│   │   ├── registry.py                  ← Three merged data-access registries:
│   │   │                                   1. DB registry (databases.toml → table meta)
│   │   │                                   2. Read-only SQLite helpers (open_readonly,
│   │   │                                      fetch_all, fetch_scalar, list_tables …)
│   │   │                                   3. TOML prompt loader (load_prompt,
│   │   │                                      get_prompt with nested key walk)
│   │   ├── helpers.py                   ← Pure stateless utility functions
│   │   │                                   safe_json_loads, truncate,
│   │   │                                   ensure_directory, read/write_text,
│   │   │                                   extract_json_from_text, pretty_print …
│   │   └── __init__.py
│   │
│   └── __init__.py
│
├── data/
│   ├── comp_db/
│   │   ├── COMP_REPORTS.db              ← Primary SQLite compliance database
│   │   ├── db_details.txt               ← Human-readable DB notes
│   │   ├── README.md
│   │   └── schema/                      ← Schema DDL files (.gitkeep)
│   ├── logs/
│   │   └── compliance_bot.log           ← Rotating log (auto-created)
│   ├── uploads/                         ← User file uploads (.gitkeep)
│   ├── reports/                         ← Generated reports (.gitkeep)
│   ├── exports/                         ← CSV exports (.gitkeep)
│   ├── cache/                           ← Query cache (.gitkeep)
│   ├── temp/                            ← Temporary files (.gitkeep)
│   ├── sample_data/                     ← Reference seed data (.gitkeep)
│   └── seed_synthetic_data.py           ← Data seeding script
│
├── tests/                               ← pytest test suite
│   ├── conftest.py

│   ├── test_intent_agent.py
```
